# SP124_RD_36 — changes against both witnesses

Counts are reading loci, not token edits. A locus differing from both witnesses contributes to both witness columns and once to its confidence column. Pure macro changes and newly added certainty marks alone are excluded from these counts.

| B008 | \uline{Proof}: First of all, we may assume that $k$ is algebraically closed: for by such a base extension, i) $\sigma$ remains free, ii) $(Y,\phi)$ remains a geometric quotient (Remark $\unsure{\gamma&#124;7}$, \S2), iii) the property of being a principal fibre bundle descends (SGA 8, 5.4). Secondly, we may replace $Y$ by one of its local rings $\operatorname{Spec}(\underline{o}_{y,Y})$, for some closed point $y\in Y$: for all conditions of Definition 0.10 are expressible locally. And thirdly, we may replace $\operatorname{Spec}(\underline{o}_{y,Y})$ by $\operatorname{Spec}(\hat{\underline{o}}_{y,Y})$ (its completion). For this is also a faithfully flat quasi-compact base extension and, as before, i) $\sigma$ remains free, ii) $(Y,\phi)$ remains a geometric quotient, and iii) the property of being a principal fibre bundle descends. | \uline{Proof}: First of all, we may assume that \(k\) is algebraically<br>closed; for by such a base extension, i) \(\sigma\) remains free, ii) \((Y,\phi)\)<br>remains a geometric quotient (Remark 7, §2), iii) the property of being<br>a principal fibre bundle descends (SGA 8, 5.4).
